> 101	const NODE_BUSY = new Set(["queued", "running"]);
> 102	function nodeMotionClass(data: CanvasNodeData): string | undefined {
> 103	  if (NODE_BUSY.has(data.status)) return "mc-node-processing";
> 104	  if (data.status === "failed") return "mc-node-failed";

> 107	function edgeMotionFor(sourceData: CanvasNodeData | undefined): "transmitting" | "established" | undefined {
> 108	  if (!sourceData) return undefined;
> 109	  if (NODE_BUSY.has(sourceData.status)) return "transmitting";
> 110	  const output = (sourceData as { output?: { generatedAt?: string } }).output;
> 111	  if (output?.generatedAt) return "established";

> 114	
> 115	/**
> 116	 * Rebuild a node from a terminal execution row (supports runs finished while the
> 117	 * canvas was closed). Returns a NEW node — the discriminated `kind` and every
> 118	 * kind-specific field survive; only `status` / `output` / `error` move.
> 119	 */
> 120	function nodeWithSuccessOutput(node: CanvasNode, exec: CanvasExecution): CanvasNode {
> 121	  const data: CanvasNode["data"] = { ...node.data };

> 140	
> 141	/** Same contract as `nodeWithSuccessOutput`, for a failed/timeout/cancelled row. */
> 142	function nodeWithFailure(node: CanvasNode, exec: CanvasExecution): CanvasNode {
> 143	  const data: CanvasNode["data"] = { ...node.data };
> 144	  data.status = "failed";
> 145	  data.error = exec.error_message ?? exec.status;
> 146	  return { ...node, data };
> 147	}
> 148	
> 149	/**
> 150	 * Per-kind gate WITHOUT the upstream-image check (for non-video kinds).
> 151	 * `imageToVideo` adds an upstream check via `findUpstreamImage` at the call site
> 152	 * inside `CanvasShell` so it has access to the current `edges` array.
> 153	 */
> 154	function isRunnableBase(node: CanvasNode): boolean {
> 155	  const d = node.data;
> 156	  if (d.kind === "characterReference") return false; // upload-only
> 157	  if (d.kind === "imageToVideo") return false;       // gated by upstream image at call site
> 158	  if (d.status === "running") return false;
> 159	  // `CanvasNode["data"]` intersects the union with `Record<string, unknown>`, so `d.prompt`
> 160	  // widens to `unknown` — narrow with typeof instead of asserting a string.
> 161	  const prompt = "prompt" in d && typeof d.prompt === "string" ? d.prompt.trim() : "";
> 162	  return prompt.length > 0;
> 163	}
> 164	
> 165	export default function CanvasEditorPage() {
> 166	  const { id: projectId } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
> 167	  const { data: project, isLoading: isProjectLoading } = useCanvasProject(projectId);
> 168	  const { data: executions, isLoading: isExecutionsLoading } = useCanvasExecutions(projectId);
> 169	  const isLoading = isProjectLoading || isExecutionsLoading;
> 170	  const initialize = useCanvasStore((s) => s.initialize);
> 171	  const currentProjectId = useCanvasStore((s) => s.projectId);
> 172	
> 173	  // Load the project graph; clear undo history after the snapshot debounce
> 174	  // settles so the initial load isn't an undo step.
> 175	  useEffect(() => {
> 176	    if (!project) return;
> 177	    
> 178	    // Reconcile nodes in the store with any new executions if project is already initialized
> 179	    if (currentProjectId === project.id) {
> 180	      if (executions && executions.length > 0) {
> 181	        const state = useCanvasStore.getState();
> 182	        let changed = false;
> 183	        const reconciledNodes = state.nodes.map((node) => {
> 184	          // Reconcile ONLY idle nodes here. A node the user just re-triggered is 'running'/'queued'
> 185	          // (in-flight) — reconciling it against `executions.find(...success)` clobbers it back to
> 186	          // 'success' using the PREVIOUS run's row, which kills the re-run motion (neon pulse + edge
> 187	          // particles never appear because the node never stays 'running'). In-flight nodes are owned
> 188	          // by useCanvasJobsRealtime + the Veo/split-screen/voice pollers; leave them alone.
> 189	          if (node.data.status === "idle") {
> 190	            const lastSuccess = executions.find(
> 191	              (e) => e.node_id === node.id && e.status === "success"
> 192	            );
> 193	            if (lastSuccess && lastSuccess.output_url) {
> 194	              changed = true;
> 195	              return nodeWithSuccessOutput(node, lastSuccess);
> 196	            }
> 197	
> 198	            const lastFailure = executions.find(
> 199	              (e) => e.node_id === node.id && (e.status === "failed" || e.status === "timeout" || e.status === "cancelled")
> 200	            );
> 201	            if (lastFailure) {
> 202	              changed = true;
> 203	              return nodeWithFailure(node, lastFailure);
> 204	            }
> 205	          }
> 206	          return node;
> 207	        });
> 208	
> 209	        if (changed) {
> 210	          useCanvasStore.setState({ nodes: reconciledNodes });
> 211	        }
> 212	      }
> 213	      return;
> 214	    }
> 215	
> 216	    const graph = project.graph ?? { nodes: [], edges: [] };
> 217	    
> 218	    // Reconcile nodes with their latest successful execution outputs (supports offline runs)
> 219	    const reconciledNodes = (graph.nodes ?? []).map((node) => {
> 220	      const lastSuccess = executions?.find(
> 221	        (e) => e.node_id === node.id && e.status === "success"
> 222	      );
> 223	      if (lastSuccess && lastSuccess.output_url) {
> 224	        return nodeWithSuccessOutput(node, lastSuccess);
> 225	      }
> 226	      return node;
> 227	    });
> 228	
> 229	    initialize(project.id, {
> 230	      nodes: reconciledNodes,
> 231	      edges: graph.edges ?? [],
> 232	      pipelineMode: graph.pipelineMode,
> 233	      lanes: graph.lanes,
> 234	      styleSettings: graph.styleSettings,
> 235	      viewport: project.viewport,
> 236	    });
> 237	    const t = setTimeout(() => useCanvasStore.temporal.getState().clear(), 600);
> 238	    return () => clearTimeout(t);
> 239	  }, [project, executions, initialize, currentProjectId]);
> 240	
> 241	  if (isLoading) {
> 242	    return (
> 243	      <div className="flex h-full items-center justify-center">
> 244	        <Loader2 className="h-8 w-8 animate-spin text-primary" />
> 245	      </div>
> 246	    );
> 247	  }
> 248	
> 249	  return (
> 250	    <>
> 251	      <Helmet>
> 252	        <title>{project?.name || "Canvas"} — Canvas Studio — MCORCH</title>
> 253	      </Helmet>
> 254	      <div className="flex h-full w-full flex-col">
> 255	        <ReactFlowProvider>
> 256	          <CanvasShell projectId={projectId} project={project ?? null} />
> 257	        </ReactFlowProvider>
> 258	      </div>
> 259	    </>
> 260	  );
> 261	}
> 262	
> 263	function CanvasShell({
> 264	  projectId,
> 265	  project,
> 266	}: {
> 267	  projectId: string | undefined;
> 268	  project: CanvasProject | null;
> 269	}) {
> 270	  useReactFlow(); // bind provider — instance used by AddNodeMenu via context
> 271	
> 272	  const nodes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes);
> 273	  const edges = useCanvasStore((s) => s.edges);
> 274	  const pipelineMode = useCanvasStore((s) => s.pipelineMode);
> 275	
> 276	  // Amendment 19 — inject motion className/data derived from REAL node state, WITHOUT
> 277	  // touching the store (getPersistedGraph must never serialize visual state).
> 278	  const motionNodes = useMemo(
> 279	    () => nodes.map((n) => {
> 280	      const cls = nodeMotionClass(n.data);
> 281	      return cls ? { ...n, className: cls } : n;
> 282	    }),
> 283	    [nodes],
> 284	  );
> 285	  const motionEdges = useMemo(() => {
> 286	    const byId = new Map(nodes.map((n) => [n.id, n.data]));
> 287	    return edges.map((e) => {
> 288	      // A terminal node (e.g. Tela Dividida) is the TARGET of its edges and has no outgoing edges,
> 289	      // so source-only derivation left its INPUT edges frozen on 'established' while it rendered.
> 290	      // When the target is busy, the edge feeding it transmits too — the re-run lights up end-to-end.
> 291	      const targetData = byId.get(e.target);
> 292	      const motion = targetData && NODE_BUSY.has(targetData.status)
> 293	        ? "transmitting"
> 294	        : edgeMotionFor(byId.get(e.source));
> 295	      return motion ? { ...e, data: { ...e.data, motion } } : e;
> 296	    });
> 297	  }, [edges, nodes]);
> 298	  const onNodesChange = useCanvasStore((s) => s.onNodesChange);
> 299	  const onEdgesChange = useCanvasStore((s) => s.onEdgesChange);
> 300	  const onConnect = useCanvasStore((s) => s.onConnect);
> 301	  const setViewport = useCanvasStore((s) => s.setViewport);
> 302	  const selectNode = useCanvasStore((s) => s.selectNode);
> 303	
> 304	  // Zoom-aware edge stroke (Sovereign 2026-08-03): edges become invisible on far
> 305	  // zoom-out because ReactFlow scales the whole SVG by transform:scale(zoom), so a
> 306	  // 1.5-2px stroke shrinks below 1px on the screen. We compensate by writing
> 307	  // clamp(1/zoom, 1, 12) into a CSS var on the wrapper — edges' strokeWidth reads
> 308	  // `calc(<base>px * var(--rf-zoom-inv))`. Zero React re-render, zero zoom clamp.
> 309	  const flowWrapperRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement>(null);
> 310	  const applyZoomCompensation = useCallback((zoom: number) => {
> 311	    const el = flowWrapperRef.current;
> 312	    if (!el) return;
> 313	    // Below 1× we scale up; at/above 1× we keep base thickness. Cap at 12× to avoid
> 314	    // blur bombs at minZoom=0.02 (would demand 50× otherwise).
> 315	    const inv = zoom >= 1 ? 1 : Math.min(12, 1 / Math.max(zoom, 0.02));
> 316	    el.style.setProperty("--rf-zoom-inv", String(inv));
> 317	  }, []);
> 318	  // Seed the CSS var from the persisted viewport before the first onMove fires,
> 319	  // so hard-refreshing at a deep zoom-out doesn't paint invisible edges for a beat.
> 320	  useEffect(() => {
> 321	    applyZoomCompensation(project?.viewport?.zoom ?? 1);
> 322	  }, [applyZoomCompensation, project?.viewport?.zoom]);
> 323	  const openConfigModal = useCanvasStore((s) => s.openConfigModal);
> 324	  const updateNodeData = useCanvasStore((s) => s.updateNodeData);
> 325	
> 326	  const autoSave = useAutoSave(projectId ?? null);
> 327	  const { profile } = useDashboardData();
> 328	  // `useDashboardData()` returns no `refetch` — destructuring one off an `as any` silently
> 329	  // produced `undefined`, so every `refetchDashboard?.()` below (and the one inside
> 330	  // useCanvasPipeline) was a no-op and the mcoCoins HUD stayed stale after a run.
> 331	  // Invalidating the hook's query key is the real refresh.
> 332	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 333	  const refetchDashboard = useCallback(() => {
> 334	    void queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["dashboardData"] });
> 335	  }, [queryClient]);
> 336	  const executeNode = useCanvasExecute();
> 337	  const updateProject = useUpdateCanvasProject();
> 338	  // it.2b: ledger-first runs live in `generations` — StatusBar sums them with the legacy counter.
> 339	  const { data: ledgerRunCount } = useProjectLedgerRunCount(projectId);
> 340	
> 341	  // Desktop: each side panel can be collapsed to give the canvas the whole width (mirrors the main
> 342	  // menu's collapse). Independent of the mobile drawers (leftOpen/rightOpen). Declared before the
> 343	  // node click handlers so they can drive the right panel (Sovereign 2026-08-03).
> 344	  const [leftCollapsed, setLeftCollapsed] = useState(false);
> 345	  const [rightCollapsed, setRightCollapsed] = useState(false);
> 346	
> 347	  const onNodeClick = useCallback(
> 348	    (_: unknown, node: Node) => {
> 349	      selectNode(node.id);
> 350	      setRightCollapsed(false); // selecting a node always surfaces its properties panel
> 351	    },
> 352	    [selectNode],
> 353	  );
> 354	
> 355	  // Amendment 31 — double-click a node opens its config in a window on the canvas (canvas>node>janela).
> 356	  const onNodeDoubleClick = useCallback(
> 357	    (_: unknown, node: Node) => {
> 358	      selectNode(node.id);
> 359	      openConfigModal();
> 360	      setRightCollapsed(true); // the config window takes over — the side panel yields
> 361	    },
> 362	    [selectNode, openConfigModal],
> 363	  );
> 364	
> 365	  const {
> 366	    pipelineStatus,
> 367	    runPipeline,
> 368	    runnableCount,
> 369	    runAllCost,
> 370	  } = useCanvasPipeline({ projectId, refetchDashboard });
> 371	
> 372	  // Phase 4.2c — Realtime bridge: webhook flips vm_canvas_executions.status,
> 373	  // we reconcile the Zustand store so the canvas node UI updates without refresh.
> 374	  useCanvasJobsRealtime(projectId, (row) => {
> 375	    if (!row.node_id) return;
> 376	    if (row.status === "success") {
> 377	      updateNodeData(row.node_id, {
> 378	        status: "success",
> 379	        output: {
> 380	          videoUrl: row.output_url ?? undefined,
> 381	          imageUrl: row.output_url ?? undefined,
> 382	          generatedAt: row.completed_at ?? new Date().toISOString(),
> 383	        },
> 384	        error: undefined,
> 385	      });
> 386	      refetchDashboard();
> 387	    } else if (
> 388	      row.status === "failed" ||
> 389	      row.status === "timeout" ||
> 390	      row.status === "cancelled"
> 391	    ) {
> 392	      updateNodeData(row.node_id, {
> 393	        status: "failed",
> 394	        error: row.error_message ?? row.status,
> 395	      });
> 396	    } else if (row.status === "queued" || row.status === "running") {
> 397	      updateNodeData(row.node_id, { status: "running" });
> 398	    }
> 399	  });
> 400	
> 401	  // Mobile: the two side panels collapse into drawers opened by floating icon buttons; the canvas + nodes
> 402	  // get the whole screen. Tapping a node auto-opens its inspector drawer. Desktop layout is untouched.
> 403	  const isMobile = useIsMobile();
> 404	  const selectedNodeId = useCanvasStore((s) => s.selectedNodeId);
> 405	  const [leftOpen, setLeftOpen] = useState(false);
> 406	  const [rightOpen, setRightOpen] = useState(false);
> 407	  useEffect(() => {
> 408	    if (isMobile && selectedNodeId) setRightOpen(true);
> 409	  }, [isMobile, selectedNodeId]);
> 410	
> 411	  return (
> 412	    <>
> 413	      <TopBar
> 414	        name={project?.name ?? "Sem título"}
> 415	        onRename={(name) => {
> 416	          if (projectId) updateProject.mutate({ projectId, name });
> 417	        }}
> 418	        saving={autoSave.status === "saving"}
> 419	        onSaveNow={() => autoSave.saveNow()}
> 420	        onRunAll={runPipeline}
> 421	        pipelineStatus={pipelineStatus}
> 422	        runnableCount={runnableCount}
> 423	        runAllCost={runAllCost}
> 424	        runAllDisabled={!projectId}
> 425	      />
> 426	
> 427	      <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">
> 428	        {!isMobile && !leftCollapsed && <LeftPanel projectId={projectId} />}
> 429	
> 430	        <div ref={flowWrapperRef} className="relative flex-1 min-w-0">
> 431	          <ReactFlow
> 432	            nodes={motionNodes}
> 433	            edges={motionEdges}
> 434	            onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
> 435	            onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
> 436	            onConnect={onConnect}
> 437	            onNodeClick={onNodeClick}
> 438	            onNodeDoubleClick={onNodeDoubleClick}
> 439	            onPaneClick={() => selectNode(null)}
> 440	            onMove={(_, vp) => applyZoomCompensation(vp.zoom)}
> 441	            onMoveEnd={(_, vp) => setViewport(vp)}
> 442	            nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
> 443	            edgeTypes={edgeTypes}
> 444	            defaultViewport={project?.viewport ?? { x: 0, y: 0, zoom: 1 }}
> 445	            minZoom={0.02}
> 446	            maxZoom={12}
> 447	            snapToGrid={pipelineMode}
> 448	            snapGrid={[COL_WIDTH, LANE_HEIGHT]}
> 449	            deleteKeyCode={["Backspace", "Delete"]}
> 450	            // fitView roda 1× no mount e SOBRESCREVE defaultViewport — para projetos que
> 451	            // já têm viewport salvo (posicionamento intencional feito pelo dono), respeitar
> 452	            // o preset. Só faz fitView em projetos novos (viewport nulo/default 0,0,1).
> 453	            fitView={!project?.viewport || (project.viewport.x === 0 && project.viewport.y === 0 && project.viewport.zoom === 1)}
> 454	            className="bg-background"
> 455	          >
> 456	            {pipelineMode && <LanesOverlay />}
> 457	            <Background gap={20} size={1} color="rgba(255,255,255,0.03)" />
> 458	            <Controls className="!border-border/50 !bg-card/80" showInteractive={false} />
> 459	            {/* Minimapa PEQUENO para priorizar o canvas com os nós; oculto no mobile (tela curta). */}
> 460	            {!isMobile && (
> 461	              <MiniMap
> 462	                className="!border-border/50 !bg-card/80"
> 463	                style={{ width: 132, height: 96 }}
> 464	                nodeColor={() => "hsl(var(--primary))"}
> 465	                maskColor="rgba(0,0,0,0.55)"
> 466	                pannable
> 467	                zoomable
> 468	              />
> 469	            )}
> 470	          </ReactFlow>
> 471	
> 472	          {/* Add-node FAB (bottom-right, icon-only no mobile) + Cmd+K palette (global) */}
> 473	          <AddNodeFab iconOnly={isMobile} />
> 474	          <AddNodeCommand />
> 475	
> 476	          {/* Desktop: colapsar/expandir as sidebars (biblioteca à esquerda · Estilo/Propriedades à direita) —
> 477	              botões flutuantes nos cantos, espelhando o colapso do menu principal. */}
> 478	          {!isMobile && (
> 479	            <>
> 480	              <Button
> 481	                type="button"
> 482	                size="icon"
> 483	                variant="secondary"
> 484	                onClick={() => setLeftCollapsed((v) => !v)}
> 485	                className="absolute left-2 top-2 z-10 h-8 w-8 rounded-md border border-border/60 shadow-sm"
> 486	                title={leftCollapsed ? "Mostrar biblioteca / receitas" : "Ocultar biblioteca / receitas"}
> 487	                aria-label={leftCollapsed ? "Mostrar biblioteca" : "Ocultar biblioteca"}
> 488	              >
> 489	                {leftCollapsed ? <PanelLeftOpen className="h-4 w-4" /> : <PanelLeftClose className="h-4 w-4" />}
> 490	              </Button>
> 491	              <Button
> 492	                type="button"
> 493	                size="icon"
> 494	                variant="secondary"
> 495	                onClick={() => setRightCollapsed((v) => !v)}
> 496	                className="absolute right-2 top-2 z-10 h-8 w-8 rounded-md border border-border/60 shadow-sm"

> 499	              >
> 500	                {rightCollapsed ? <PanelRightOpen className="h-4 w-4" /> : <PanelRightClose className="h-4 w-4" />}
> 501	              </Button>
> 502	            </>
> 503	          )}
> 504	
> 505	          {/* Mobile: FABs redondos só-ícone para abrir as sidebars colapsadas. */}
> 506	          {isMobile && (

> 508	              <Button

> 518	              </Button>

> 521	                size="icon"
> 522	                variant="secondary"
> 523	                onClick={() => setRightOpen(true)}

> 529	              </Button>

> 534	        {/* Typed inspectors panel — right column on desktop; a drawer on mobile (below). */}
> 535	        {!isMobile && !rightCollapsed && (

> 544	      {isMobile && (

> 246	
> 247	export function searchRegistry(q: string): NodeRegistryEntry[] {
> 248	  const s = q.trim().toLowerCase();
> 249	  if (!s) return CANVAS_NODE_REGISTRY;
> 250	  return CANVAS_NODE_REGISTRY.filter(
> 251	    (e) =>
> 252	      e.label.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
> 253	      e.description.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
> 254	      e.category.toLowerCase().includes(s) ||
> 255	      e.aliases.some((a) => a.includes(s)),
> 256	  );
> 257	}
> 258	
> ```

> 2	import { useCanvasStore } from "@/store/useCanvasStore";
> 3	import { CANVAS_NODE_REGISTRY } from "@/lib/canvas-node-registry";
> 4	import { NodeInspectorBody } from "./RightPanel/NodeInspectorBody";
> 5	
> 6	interface NodeConfigWindowProps {
> 7	  projectId: string;
> 8	  creditsBalance: number;
> 9	  onCreditsChanged: () => void;
> 10	}
> 11	
> 12	// Amendment 31 — a node's heavy customization/config surface, opened as a WINDOW on the canvas
> 13	// (canvas>node>janela). Renders the SAME NodeInspectorBody as the docked sidebar, but roomier. The
> 14	// sidebar yields to a placeholder while this is open, so the kind-specific inspector mounts once.
> 15	export function NodeConfigWindow({ projectId, creditsBalance, onCreditsChanged }: NodeConfigWindowProps) {
> 16	  const open = useCanvasStore((s) => s.configModalOpen);
> 17	  const closeConfigModal = useCanvasStore((s) => s.closeConfigModal);
> 18	  const selectedId = useCanvasStore((s) => s.selectedNodeId);
> 19	  const node = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes.find((n) => n.id === selectedId) ?? null);
> 20	  const label = node
> 21	    ? CANVAS_NODE_REGISTRY.find((e) => e.id === node.data.kind)?.label ?? node.data.kind
> 22	    : "";
> 23	
> 24	  return (
> 25	    <Dialog open={open && !!node} onOpenChange={(o) => { if (!o) closeConfigModal(); }}>
> 26	      <DialogContent className="max-w-2xl gap-0 overflow-hidden p-0">
> 27	        <DialogHeader className="border-b border-border px-4 py-3">
> 28	          <DialogTitle className="text-sm font-medium">Personalizar · {label}</DialogTitle>
> 29	        </DialogHeader>
> 30	        <div className="h-[72vh] overflow-hidden">
> 31	          {node && (
> 32	            <NodeInspectorBody
> 33	              node={node}
> 34	              projectId={projectId}
> 35	              creditsBalance={creditsBalance}
> 36	              onCreditsChanged={onCreditsChanged}
> 37	            />
> 38	          )}
> 39	        </div>
> 40	      </DialogContent>
> 41	    </Dialog>
> 42	  );
> 43	}
> 44	
> ```

> 152	export function useSaveCanvasGraph() {
> 153	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 154	
> 155	  return useMutation({
> 156	    mutationFn: async ({ projectId, graph, viewport }: {
> 157	      projectId: string;
> 158	      graph: PersistedCanvasGraph;
> 159	      viewport?: { x: number; y: number; zoom: number };
> 160	    }) => {
> 161	      // Hydration-race guard (data-loss fix): NEVER overwrite a non-empty saved graph with an empty one.
> 162	      // useAutoSave can flush (debounce / pagehide / unmount) BEFORE initialize() hydrates the store, so the
> 163	      // empty boot state {nodes:[]} would clobber a rich DB row → all canvas nodes lost (assets survive in
> 164	      // vm_canvas_executions, which is why the project still shows assets but no nodes). When the incoming
> 165	      // graph is empty, read-before-write and skip the clobber (viewport-only updates still persist).
> 166	      if (graph.nodes.length === 0) {
> 167	        const { data: existing } = await supabase
> 168	          .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 169	          .select("graph")
> 170	          .eq("id", projectId)
> 171	          .maybeSingle();
> 172	        const existingNodes = ((existing?.graph as unknown as PersistedCanvasGraph | null)?.nodes) ?? [];
> 173	        if (existingNodes.length > 0) {
> 174	          console.warn(
> 175	            `[saveCanvasGraph] blocked empty-graph overwrite of ${existingNodes.length}-node project ${projectId} (hydration race)`,
> 176	          );
> 177	          if (viewport) {
> 178	            await supabase.from("vm_canvas_projects").update({ viewport }).eq("id", projectId);
> 179	          }
> 180	          return;
> 181	        }
> 182	      }
> 183	
> 184	      // `graph` é coluna jsonb → a linha Update a tipa como `Json`. `PersistedCanvasGraph` é uma
> 185	      // interface (e carrega Node/Edge do @xyflow, também interfaces), e o TypeScript não concede index
> 186	      // signature implícita a interfaces: o blob não é ESTRUTURALMENTE assignável a `Json` mesmo sendo
> 187	      // JSON-serializável de fato (é exatamente o que o supabase-js serializa no wire). O alargamento
> 188	      // fica CONFINADO a este campo e tipado no destino; tipar `updateData` como TablesUpdate mantém o
> 189	      // resto do update checado contra o schema — o `as any` anterior aceitaria qualquer coluna
> 190	      // inexistente (um typo tipo `{ statu: "x" }`) sem erro nenhum.
> 191	      const updateData: TablesUpdate<"vm_canvas_projects"> = { graph: graph as unknown as Json };
> 192	      if (viewport) updateData.viewport = viewport;
> 193	
> 194	      const { error } = await supabase
> 195	        .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 196	        .update(updateData)
> 197	        .eq("id", projectId);
> 198	      if (error) throw error;
> 199	    },
> 200	    onSuccess: (_, vars) => {
> 201	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-project", vars.projectId] });
> 202	    },
> 203	  });
> 204	}

> 223	// ── Canvas Execute ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 224	
> 225	// it.2b (Amendment 14 FR-SPACES-024): sync node types run LEDGER-FIRST — a fresh node_run_id per
> 226	// attempt dispatches canvas-execute into the `generations` branch (begin→provider→finalize, atomic
> 227	// debit + automatic refund). image_to_video stays on the legacy path until it.2c wires the async
> 228	// settle + watchdog. SOP: docs/processes/spaces-graph-execution.md §Amendment it.2a.
> 229	const LEDGER_SYNC_NODE_TYPES = new Set([
> 230	  "generate_image",
> 231	  "magic_prompt",
> 232	  "scene_compose",
> 233	  "style_transfer",
> 234	  "upscale",
> 235	]);
> 236	
> 237	/**
> 238	 * Amendment 25 (FR-SPACES-086/090): o motor Veo é ledger-first por CONSTRUÇÃO (debita no submit,
> 239	 * estorna integralmente na falha, o poll finaliza). Sem `node_run_id` o `canvas-execute` despacha
> 240	 * para a branch LEGADA — que não conhece `google/veo-*` e devolveria 422: a feature nasceria morta.
> 241	 *
> 242	 * O gate é por PROVIDER, não pelo node_type: o vídeo do Higgsfield continua exatamente no caminho
> 243	 * legado que já está em produção (webhook `token=`), sem mudança de comportamento. Só o Veo entra
> 244	 * no `generations`.
> 245	 */
> 246	function needsLedgerRun(nodeType: string, provider: string): boolean {
> 247	  if (LEDGER_SYNC_NODE_TYPES.has(nodeType)) return true;
> 248	  return nodeType === "image_to_video" && provider === "google";
> 249	}
> 250	
> 251	export function useCanvasExecute() {
> 252	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 253	
> 254	  return useMutation({
> 255	    mutationFn: async (params: {
> 256	      project_id: string;
> 257	      node_id: string;
> 258	      node_type: string;
> 259	      provider: string;
> 260	      model: string;
> 261	      prompt: string;
> 262	      negative_prompt?: string;
> 263	      parameters?: Record<string, unknown>;
> 264	      /** Phase 4.2c — required for image_to_video (upstream image URL). */
> 265	      input_asset_url?: string;
> 266	      input_asset_id?: string;
> 267	      input_image_1?: string;
> 268	      input_image_2?: string;
> 269	      input_layout?: string;
> 270	      /** Phase 4.3 — cross-node consistency: upstream connected images as references. */
> 271	      reference_image_urls?: string[];
> 272	      /** Amendment 20 (FR-SPACES-072): qual chave do pool BYOK usar (user_provider_keys.id, owner-validado server-side). */
> 273	      provider_key_id?: string;
> 274	    }) => {
> 275	      // Fresh run id per ATTEMPT (never derived from node_id): retries are new runs; the id's only
> 276	      // job is making network replays of the SAME attempt idempotent server-side (S2/G2).
> 277	      const body = needsLedgerRun(params.node_type, params.provider)
> 278	        ? { ...params, node_run_id: crypto.randomUUID() }
> 279	        : params;
> 280	      const { data, error } = await supabase.functions.invoke("canvas-execute", { body });
> 281	      if (error) {
> 282	        let message = error.message;
> 283	        try {
> 284	          const ctx = (error as { context?: Response }).context;
> 285	          const parsed = ctx ? await ctx.json() : null;
> 286	          // Prefer the pt-BR message (ledger 402/422/502 bodies carry it); fall back to the slug.
> 287	          if (parsed?.message) message = parsed.message as string;
> 288	          else if (parsed?.error) message = parsed.error as string;
> 289	        } catch {
> 290	          // Fall back to original error message
> 291	        }
> 292	        throw new Error(message);
> 293	      }
> 294	      return data;
> 295	    },
> 296	    onSuccess: (_, vars) => {
> 297	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-project", vars.project_id] });
> 298	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-assets"] });
> 299	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-executions"] });
> 300	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["project-ledger-runs", vars.project_id] });
> 301	    },
> 302	  });
> 303	}
> 304	
> 305	// ── Assets & Executions ──────────────────────────────────────────────
> 306	
> 307	export function useCanvasAssets(projectId?: string) {
> 308	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 309	
> 310	  return useQuery({
> 311	    queryKey: ["vm-canvas-assets", projectId, user?.id],
> 312	    queryFn: async () => {
> 313	      if (!user) return [];
> 314	      let query = supabase
> 315	        .from("vm_canvas_assets")
> 316	        .select("*")
> 317	        .eq("user_id", user.id)
> 318	        .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
> 319	        .limit(50);
> 320	
> 321	      if (projectId) query = query.eq("project_id", projectId);
> 322	
> 323	      const { data, error } = await query;
> 324	      if (error) throw error;
> 325	      return (data || []) as unknown as CanvasAsset[];
> 326	    },
> 327	    enabled: !!user,
> 328	  });
> 329	}
> 330	
> 331	/** it.2b — normalize a `generations` ledger row into the CanvasExecution display shape. */
> 332	function ledgerRowToExecution(g: {
> 333	  id: string; project_id: string | null; node_id: string; model_key: string;
> 334	  output_type: string; status: string; mco_charged: number; result: unknown; created_at: string;
> 335	}): CanvasExecution {
> 336	  const slash = g.model_key.indexOf("/");
> 337	  const nodeType = g.output_type === "text"
> 338	    ? "magic_prompt"
> 339	    : (g.model_key === "openrouter/scene-compose"
> 340	      ? "scene_compose"
> 341	      : (g.model_key === "openrouter/style-transfer" ? "style_transfer" : "generate_image"));
> 342	  return {
> 343	    id: g.id,
> 344	    project_id: g.project_id ?? "",
> 345	    node_id: g.node_id,
> 346	    node_type: nodeType,
> 347	    provider: slash > 0 ? g.model_key.slice(0, slash) : g.model_key,
> 348	    model: slash > 0 ? g.model_key.slice(slash + 1) : g.model_key,
> 349	    prompt: null,
> 350	    status: g.status === "done" ? "success" : (g.status === "error" ? "failed" : g.status),
> 351	    output_url: (g.result as { output_url?: string } | null)?.output_url ?? null,
> 352	    credits_charged: Number(g.mco_charged),
> 353	    error_message: null,
> 354	    created_at: g.created_at,
> 355	  };
> 356	}
> 357	
> 358	export function useCanvasExecutions(projectId?: string) {
> 359	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 360	
> 361	  return useQuery({
> 362	    queryKey: ["vm-canvas-executions", projectId, user?.id],
> 363	    queryFn: async () => {
> 364	      if (!user) return [];
> 365	      let legacyQuery = supabase
> 366	        .from("vm_canvas_executions")
> 367	        .select("*")
> 368	        .eq("user_id", user.id)
> 369	        .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
> 370	        .limit(30);
> 371	      if (projectId) legacyQuery = legacyQuery.eq("project_id", projectId);
> 372	
> 373	      // it.2b dual-read: ledger-first runs live in `generations` (project surface only — space
> 374	      // rows belong to the retired v1 UI). Union + merge-sort until the legacy ledger retires.
> 375	      let ledgerQuery = supabase
> 376	        .from("generations")
> 377	        .select("id, project_id, node_id, model_key, output_type, status, mco_charged, result, created_at")
> 378	        .eq("owner_id", user.id)
> 379	        .not("project_id", "is", null)
> 380	        .order("created_at", { ascending: false })
> 381	        .limit(30);
> 382	      if (projectId) ledgerQuery = ledgerQuery.eq("project_id", projectId);
> 383	
> 384	      const [legacy, ledger] = await Promise.all([legacyQuery, ledgerQuery]);
> 385	      if (legacy.error) throw legacy.error;
> 386	      if (ledger.error) throw ledger.error;
> 387	
> 388	      const merged = [
> 389	        ...((legacy.data || []) as CanvasExecution[]),
> 390	        ...((ledger.data || []) as Parameters<typeof ledgerRowToExecution>[0][]).map(ledgerRowToExecution),
> 391	      ].sort((a, b) => (a.created_at < b.created_at ? 1 : -1));
> 392	      return merged.slice(0, 30);
> 393	    },
> 394	    enabled: !!user,
> 395	  });
> 396	}
> 397	
> 398	/** it.2b — ledger run count for a project (StatusBar sums it with the legacy total_executions). */
> 399	export function useProjectLedgerRunCount(projectId?: string) {
> 400	  const { user } = useAuth();
> 401	  return useQuery({
> 402	    queryKey: ["project-ledger-runs", projectId, user?.id],
> 403	    queryFn: async () => {
> 404	      if (!user || !projectId) return 0;
> 405	      const { count, error } = await supabase
> 406	        .from("generations")
> 407	        .select("id", { count: "exact", head: true })
> 408	        .eq("owner_id", user.id)
> 409	        .eq("project_id", projectId);
> 410	      if (error) throw error;
> 411	      return count ?? 0;
> 412	    },
> 413	    enabled: !!user && !!projectId,
> 414	  });
> 415	}
> 416	
> 417	// ── Recipes ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 418	
> 419	export interface CanvasRecipe {
> 420	  id: string;
> 421	  user_id: string | null;
> 422	  name: string;
> 423	  description: string | null;
> 424	  thumbnail_url: string | null;
> 425	  graph: CanvasGraph;
> 426	  estimated_credits: number;
> 427	  category: string | null;
> 428	  tags: string[];
> 429	  is_public: boolean;
> 430	  is_official: boolean;
> 431	  uses_count: number;
> 432	  created_at: string;
> 433	}
> 434	
> 435	/** Public + own recipes. RLS gates the result to is_public OR auth.uid() = user_id. */
> 436	export function useCanvasRecipes() {
> 437	  return useQuery({
> 438	    queryKey: ["vm-canvas-recipes"],
> 439	    queryFn: async () => {
> 440	      const { data, error } = await supabase
> 441	        .from("vm_canvas_recipes")
> 442	        .select("*")
> 443	        .order("is_official", { ascending: false })
> 444	        .order("uses_count", { ascending: false });
> 445	      if (error) throw error;
> 446	      return (data || []) as unknown as CanvasRecipe[];
> 447	    },
> 448	    staleTime: 5 * 60 * 1000,
> 449	  });
> 450	}
> 451	
> 452	// ── Project metadata ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
> 453	
> 454	export function useUpdateCanvasProject() {
> 455	  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
> 456	
> 457	  return useMutation({
> 458	    mutationFn: async ({ projectId, name, description }: {
> 459	      projectId: string;
> 460	      name?: string;
> 461	      description?: string;
> 462	    }) => {
> 463	      const patch: Record<string, string> = {};
> 464	      if (name !== undefined) patch.name = name;
> 465	      if (description !== undefined) patch.description = description;
> 466	      const { error } = await supabase
> 467	        .from("vm_canvas_projects")
> 468	        .update(patch)
> 469	        .eq("id", projectId);
> 470	      if (error) throw error;
> 471	    },
> 472	    onSuccess: (_, vars) => {
> 473	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-project", vars.projectId] });
> 474	      queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ["vm-canvas-projects"] });
> 475	    },
> 476	  });
> 477	}
> 478	
> ```

> 247	        if (allDone) {
> 248	          unsubscribe();
> 249	          resolve(!anyFailed);
> 250	        }
> 251	      });
> 252	    });
> 253	  }, []);
> 254	
> 255	  // Execute pipeline
> 256	  const runPipeline = useCallback(async () => {
> 257	    if (!projectId) return;
> 258	
> 259	    let layers: CanvasNode[][];
> 260	    try {
> 261	      layers = getTopologicalLayers(nodes, edges);
> 262	    } catch (e) {
> 263	      setPipelineStatus("blocked");
> 264	      toast.error("Ciclo detectado no grafo! O pipeline não pode ser executado.");
> 265	      return;
> 266	    }
> 267	
> 268	    const runnableIds = runnable.map((n) => n.id);
> 269	    if (runnableIds.length === 0) {
> 270	      toast.error("Nenhum nó qualificado para execução.");
> 271	      return;
> 272	    }
> 273	
> 274	    setPipelineStatus("running");
> 275	    toast.info(`Iniciando execução em camadas de ${runnableIds.length} nós...`);
> 276	
> 277	    let executionSuccess = true;
> 278	
> 279	    for (let i = 0; i < layers.length; i++) {
> 280	      const layer = layers[i];
> 281	      const layerRunnable = layer.filter((n) => runnableIds.includes(n.id));
> 282	
> 283	      if (layerRunnable.length === 0) {
> 284	        continue;
> 285	      }
> 286	
> 287	      // Mark layer nodes as queued
> 288	      for (const n of layerRunnable) {
> 289	        updateNodeData(n.id, { status: "queued", error: undefined });
> 290	      }
> 291	
> 292	      // Wait 100ms for UI status update
> 293	      await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 100));
> 294	
> 295	      // Execute all nodes in this layer in parallel
> 296	      const runPromises = layerRunnable.map((n) => runSingleNode(n.id));
> 297	      await Promise.all(runPromises);
> 298	
> 299	      // Wait for all nodes in this layer to reach success/failed
> 300	      const layerIds = layerRunnable.map((n) => n.id);
> 301	      const layerSuccess = await waitForNodesToComplete(layerIds);
> 302	
> 303	      if (!layerSuccess) {
> 304	        executionSuccess = false;
> 305	        break;
> 306	      }
> 307	    }
> 308	
> 309	    if (executionSuccess) {
> 310	      setPipelineStatus("done");
> 311	      toast.success("Pipeline executado com sucesso!");
> 312	    } else {
> 313	      setPipelineStatus("error");
> 314	      toast.error("Erro na execução do pipeline. Algumas etapas falharam.");
> 315	    }
> 316	
> 317	    refetchDashboard?.();
> 318	  }, [nodes, edges, runnable, projectId, runSingleNode, waitForNodesToComplete, updateNodeData, refetchDashboard]);
> 319	
> 320	  const resetPipeline = useCallback(() => {
> 321	    setPipelineStatus("pending");
> 322	  }, []);
> 323	
> 324	  return {
> 325	    pipelineStatus,
> 326	    runPipeline,
> 327	    resetPipeline,
> 328	    runnable,
> 329	    runnableCount,
> 330	    runAllCost,
> 331	    runSingleNode,
> 332	  };
> 333	}
> 334	
> ```

>
> ```
> 1	import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from "react";
> 2	import { useCanvasStore } from "@/store/useCanvasStore";
> 3	import { useSaveCanvasGraph } from "@/hooks/useCanvasStudio";
> 4	
> 5	export type AutoSaveStatus = "idle" | "pending" | "saving" | "saved" | "error";
> 6	
> 7	/** Debounce window between last edit and persistence (ms). */
> 8	const AUTOSAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS = 3_000;
> 9	
> 10	/**
> 11	 * Debounced auto-save: persists the canvas graph ~1 min after the last
> 12	 * change, and flushes immediately on tab hide / unmount so a pending edit
> 13	 * is never dropped. pipelineMode / lanes / styleSettings ride inside the
> 14	 * graph jsonb blob — no separate settings write.
> 15	 */
> 16	export function useAutoSave(projectId: string | null) {
> 17	  const nodes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.nodes);
> 18	  const edges = useCanvasStore((s) => s.edges);
> 19	  const viewport = useCanvasStore((s) => s.viewport);
> 20	  const pipelineMode = useCanvasStore((s) => s.pipelineMode);
> 21	  const lanes = useCanvasStore((s) => s.lanes);
> 22	  const styleSettings = useCanvasStore((s) => s.styleSettings);
> 23	  const saveGraph = useSaveCanvasGraph();
> 24	
> 25	  const [status, setStatus] = useState<AutoSaveStatus>("idle");
> 26	  const [lastSaved, setLastSaved] = useState<Date | null>(null);
> 27	  const firstRun = useRef(true);
> 28	
> 29	  // Reassigned every render so it always closes over the latest mutation.
> 30	  const flush = useRef(async () => {});
> 31	  flush.current = async () => {
> 32	    if (!projectId) return;
> 33	    setStatus("saving");
> 34	    try {
> 35	      const state = useCanvasStore.getState();
> 36	      await saveGraph.mutateAsync({
> 37	        projectId,
> 38	        graph: state.getPersistedGraph(),
> 39	        viewport: state.viewport,
> 40	      });
> 41	      setStatus("saved");
> 42	      setLastSaved(new Date());
> 43	    } catch (e) {
> 44	      console.error("[autoSave] failed", e);
> 45	      setStatus("error");
> 46	    }
> 47	  };
> 48	
> 49	  /** Force a save immediately, bypassing the debounce. */
> 50	  const saveNow = async () => {
> 51	    await flush.current();
> 52	  };
> 53	
> 54	  useEffect(() => {
> 55	    if (!projectId) return;
> 56	    if (firstRun.current) {
> 57	      firstRun.current = false;
> 58	      return;
> 59	    }
> 60	    setStatus("pending");
> 61	    const t = setTimeout(() => {
> 62	      flush.current();
> 63	    }, AUTOSAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS);
> 64	    return () => clearTimeout(t);
> 65	  }, [projectId, nodes, edges, viewport, pipelineMode, lanes, styleSettings]);
> 66	
> 67	  // Flush on tab hide / unmount so the 1-minute window can't drop edits.
> 68	  useEffect(() => {
> 69	    if (!projectId) return;
> 70	    const onHide = () => {
> 71	      if (document.visibilityState === "hidden") flush.current();
> 72	    };
> 73	    const onPageHide = () => flush.current();
> 74	    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onHide);
> 75	    window.addEventListener("pagehide", onPageHide);
> 76	    return () => {
> 77	      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onHide);
> 78	      window.removeEventListener("pagehide", onPageHide);
> 79	      flush.current();
> 80	    };
> 81	  }, [projectId]);
> 82	
> 83	  return { status, lastSaved, saveNow };
> 84	}
> 85	
> ```

> 205	async function streamViaEdgeFunction(
> 206	  jwt: string,
> 207	  model: string,
> 208	  system: string,
> 209	  history: AIOSMessage[],
> 210	  userContent: string,
> 211	  assistantId: string,
> 212	  setMessages: React.Dispatch<React.SetStateAction<AIOSMessage[]>>,
> 213	  onTokens: ((d: string) => void) | undefined,
> 214	  signal: AbortController,
> 215	) {
> 216	  const msgs = [
> 217	    ...history.filter((m) => !m.isStreaming).map((m) => ({ role: m.role, content: m.content })),
> 218	    { role: "user", content: userContent },
> 219	  ];
> 220	
> 221	  const supabaseUrl = import.meta.env.VITE_SUPABASE_URL as string;
> 222	  const res = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/aios-chat-stream`, {
> 223	    method: "POST",
> 224	    headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${jwt}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
> 225	    body: JSON.stringify({ messages: msgs, system, model }),
> 226	    signal: signal.signal,
> 227	  });
> 228	
> 229	  if (!res.ok) {
> 230	    const body = await res.json().catch(() => ({}));
> 231	    throw new Error(body?.error ?? `Erro AIOS: ${res.status}`);
> 232	  }
> 233	
> 234	  const reader = res.body?.getReader();
> 235	  if (!reader) return;
> 236	  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
> 237	  let accumulated = "";
> 238	
> 239	  while (true) {
> 240	    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
> 241	    if (done) break;
> 242	    const chunk = decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
> 243	    for (const line of chunk.split("\n")) {
> 244	      if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;
> 245	      const json = line.slice(6).trim();
> 246	      if (json === "[DONE]") break;
> 247	      try {
> 248	        const parsed = JSON.parse(json);
> 249	        const delta = parsed?.choices?.[0]?.delta?.content ?? "";
> 250	        if (delta) {
> 251	          accumulated += delta;
> 252	          onTokens?.(delta);
> 253	          setMessages((prev) =>
> 254	            prev.map((m) => (m.id === assistantId ? { ...m, content: accumulated } : m)),
> 255	          );
> 256	        }
> 257	      } catch { /* ignore malformed chunks */ }
> 258	    }
> 259	  }
> 260	}

> ```
> 1	import { BaseEdge, getBezierPath, type EdgeProps } from "@xyflow/react";
> 2	
> 3	/**
> 4	 * Default edge of the Spaces canvas with state-driven motion (Amendment 19).
> 5	 * Motion is TELEMETRY, never decoration — `data.motion` is derived by the
> 6	 * CanvasEditorPage from the REAL source-node state:
> 7	 *   'transmitting' — source is queued/running → neon particles travel source→target
> 8	 *   'established'  — source delivered a real output → the wire glows and breathes
> 9	 *   undefined      — quiet default (same visual weight edges had before)
> 10	 * Technique: BaseEdge + getBezierPath (precedents: OrchestrationEdge, PipelineEdge);
> 11	 * particles ride the SVG path via <animateMotion><mpath> referencing the path id.
> 12	 */
> 13	export type EdgeMotion = "transmitting" | "established" | undefined;
> 14	
> 15	export function FlowEdge(props: EdgeProps) {
> 16	  const {
> 17	    id,
> 18	    sourceX,
> 19	    sourceY,
> 20	    targetX,
> 21	    targetY,
> 22	    sourcePosition,
> 23	    targetPosition,
> 24	    markerEnd,
> 25	    selected,
> 26	    data,
> 27	  } = props;
> 28	
> 29	  const motion = (data as { motion?: EdgeMotion } | undefined)?.motion;
> 30	
> 31	  const [path] = getBezierPath({
> 32	    sourceX,
> 33	    sourceY,
> 34	    sourcePosition,
> 35	    targetX,
> 36	    targetY,
> 37	    targetPosition,
> 38	  });
> 39	
> 40	  // The mpath needs a concrete SVG id — BaseEdge already renders its path with props.id,
> 41	  // but ReactFlow edge ids may contain characters that are awkward in url()/href refs, so
> 42	  // particles reference a dedicated invisible twin path with a sanitized id.
> 43	  const pathId = `mc-flow-${id.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9_-]/g, "_")}`;
> 44	
> 45	  const stroke =
> 46	    motion === "established" ? "#00F2FF"
> 47	    : motion === "transmitting" ? "rgba(0, 242, 255, 0.55)"
> 48	    : "hsl(var(--border))";
> 49	
> 50	  return (
> 51	    <>
> 52	      <BaseEdge
> 53	        id={id}
> 54	        path={path}
> 55	        markerEnd={markerEnd}
> 56	        style={{
> 57	          stroke,
> 58	          // Zoom-aware stroke: CanvasEditorPage sets --rf-zoom-inv on the wrapper
> 59	          // to clamp(1/zoom, 1, 12), so the wire stays visible on far zoom-out
> 60	          // without swelling when zoomed in. Zero React re-render (CSS-only).
> 61	          strokeWidth: `calc(${(motion ? 2 : 1.5) * (selected ? 1.4 : 1)}px * var(--rf-zoom-inv, 1))`,
> 62	          strokeDasharray: motion === "transmitting" ? "7 5" : undefined,
> 63	          opacity: motion ? 1 : 0.7,
> 64	          transition: "stroke 0.3s ease, opacity 0.3s ease",
> 65	        }}
> 66	        className={
> 67	          motion === "transmitting" ? "mc-edge-transmitting"
> 68	          : motion === "established" ? "mc-edge-established"
> 69	          : undefined
> 70	        }
> 71	      />
> 72	      {motion === "transmitting" && (
> 73	        <>
> 74	          {/* Invisible twin path for the particles' motion track. */}
> 75	          <path id={pathId} d={path} fill="none" stroke="none" />
> 76	          {[0, 0.8].map((delay) => (
> 77	            <circle
> 78	              key={delay}
> 79	              r={3}
> 80	              fill="#00F2FF"
> 81	              className="mc-edge-particle"
> 82	              style={{ filter: "drop-shadow(0 0 4px rgba(0, 242, 255, 0.9))" }}
> 83	            >
> 84	              <animateMotion dur="1.6s" begin={`${delay}s`} repeatCount="indefinite">
> 85	                <mpath href={`#${pathId}`} />
> 86	              </animateMotion>
> 87	            </circle>
> 88	          ))}
> 89	        </>
> 90	      )}
> 91	    </>
> 92	  );
> 93	}
> 94	
> ```

> /* AIOS Kanban card "em execução" (status in_progress/running) — mesma linguagem de motion
>    dos nós do Spaces (glow cyan #00F2FF + anel conic girando), derivada de .mc-node-processing
>    mas afinada para o card do Kanban: o card é o PRÓPRIO alvo (não um wrapper), tem rounded-xl
>    (0.75rem, não 0.5rem) e nasce com overflow-hidden (cortaria o anel em inset:-2px).
>    Caveats resolvidos: (1) overflow visível p/ o anel não ser cortado; (2) isolation:isolate cria
>    stacking context próprio para o ::before em z-index:-1 abraçar o card em vez de sumir atrás do
>    fundo da coluna (bg-card/40); (3) raio do anel = raio do card + 2px. */
> .mc-kanban-card-running {
>   border-radius: 0.75rem; /* matches Tailwind rounded-xl on the card */
>   overflow: visible !important; /* card has overflow-hidden; the ring lives at inset:-2px */
>   isolation: isolate; /* own stacking context so ::before z-index:-1 hugs the card, not the column bg */
>   animation: mc-pulse-glow 1.8s ease-in-out infinite;
> }
> .mc-kanban-card-running::before {
>   content: "";
>   position: absolute;
>   inset: -2px;
>   z-index: -1;
>   border-radius: 0.875rem; /* card radius (0.75rem) + 2px inset */
>   padding: 2px;
>   background: conic-gradient(
>     from var(--mc-spin),
>     transparent 0deg,
>     rgba(0, 242, 255, 0.05) 40deg,
>     #00f2ff 90deg,
>     rgba(0, 242, 255, 0.05) 140deg,
>     transparent 180deg,
>     transparent 360deg
>   );
>   -webkit-mask: linear-gradient(#000 0 0) content-box, linear-gradient(#000 0 0);
>   -webkit-mask-composite: xor;
>   mask-composite: exclude;
>   animation: mc-node-ring-spin 1.6s linear infinite;
>   pointer-events: none;
> }
>

> /* Acessibilidade: preferências de movimento reduzido desligam o motion do canvas

>   .mc-node-processing,

>   .mc-kanban-card-running,
>   .mc-kanban-card-running::before,
>   .mc-node-failed,
>   .mc-edge-transmitting,
>   .mc-edge-established,
>   .mc-edge-particle {
>     animation: none !important;
>   }
> }

> /** S19 — async video poller: reads the node's own `generations` row (RLS own) every POLL_MS until
>  *  terminal, then flips the node + invalidates the cluster. Timeout leaves the node `running`
>  *  honestly (the S17 sweep is the money backstop). */
> const POLL_MS = 5_000;
> const POLL_MAX_MS = 12 * 60_000;
> function watchVideoGeneration(
>   nodeId: string,
>   nodeRunId: string,
>   spaceId: string,
>   onSettled: (status: 'done' | 'error', refunded: number) => void,
> ) {
>   const startedAt = Date.now();
>   const tick = async () => {
>     try {
>       const { data } = await supabase
>         .from('generations')
>         .select('status, mco_refunded')
>         .eq('node_run_id', nodeRunId)
>         .maybeSingle();
>       const status = data?.status;
>       if (status === 'done' || status === 'error') {
>         onSettled(status, Number(data?.mco_refunded) || 0);
>         return;
>       }
>     } catch { /* transient read error — keep polling */ }
>     if (Date.now() - startedAt < POLL_MAX_MS) {
>       setTimeout(tick, POLL_MS);
>     } else {
>       toast({
>         title: 'Vídeo ainda processando',
>         description: 'O cluster do nó atualiza quando concluir; execuções travadas são estornadas automaticamente.',
>       });
>     }
>   };
>   setTimeout(tick, POLL_MS);
> }
> 
> /** Structured run failure — kind drives node status + run control (FRD error table 04:100-107). */
> class RunNodeError extends Error {
>   kind: 'blocked' | 'error' | 'abort';
>   constructor(kind: 'blocked' | 'error' | 'abort', message: string) {
>     super(message);
>     this.kind = kind;
>   }
> }
> 
> /** Extract {status, code, message} from a FunctionsHttpError and map to run semantics. */
> async function toRunError(error: unknown): Promise<RunNodeError> {
>   const ctx = (error as { context?: Response })?.context;
>   let status = 0;
>   let code = '';
>   let message = '';
>   try {
>     status = (ctx as Response | undefined)?.status ?? 0;
>     const body = await (ctx as Response | undefined)?.json?.();
>     code = (body?.error as string) ?? '';
>     message = (body?.message as string) ?? code;
>   } catch { /* body was not JSON */ }
> 
>   if (status === 402) {
>     const daily = /daily|cap/i.test(code);
>     return new RunNodeError('blocked', daily
>       ? 'Teto diário de mcoCoins do dia já atingido.'
>       : 'Saldo de mcoCoins insuficiente.');
>   }
>   if (status === 404) return new RunNodeError('abort', 'Space não encontrado.');
>   if (status === 422) return new RunNodeError('error', message || 'Parâmetros inválidos para este nó.');
>   if (status === 502) return new RunNodeError('error', message || 'Falha do provedor — o valor debitado foi estornado.');
>   return new RunNodeError('error', message || (error as Error)?.message || 'Falha na execução do nó.');
> }
> 
> /** Seed the in-run output map with each node's LATEST done generation (S11 threading across runs). */
> async function seedOutputs(spaceId: string, outputs: Map<string, NodeOutput>) {
>   const { data } = await supabase
>     .from('generations')
>     .select('node_id, output_type, result, created_at')
>     .eq('space_id', spaceId)
>     .eq('status', 'done')
>     .order('created_at', { ascending: false })
>     .limit(200);
>   for (const row of data ?? []) {
>     if (outputs.has(row.node_id)) continue; // newest wins (ordered DESC)
>     const result = (row.result ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown>;
>     if (row.output_type === 'text' && typeof result.enhanced_prompt === 'string') {
>       outputs.set(row.node_id, { type: 'text', value: result.enhanced_prompt });
>     } else if (row.output_type === 'image' && typeof result.output_url === 'string') {
>       outputs.set(row.node_id, { type: 'image', value: result.output_url });
>     }
>   }
> }
>

>     10	export interface CanvasExecutionRow {

>     12	  project_id: string;
>     13	  node_id: string | null;
>     14	  status: 'pending' | 'queued' | 'running' | 'success' | 'failed' | 'cancelled' | 'timeout';

>     17	  completed_at: string | null;
>     18	}

>     20	/**
>     21	 * Subscribes to vm_canvas_executions UPDATEs for the current user via Supabase Realtime.
>     22	 * Invalidates TanStack Query keys on every status transition AND invokes the optional
>     23	 * `onUpdate` callback so callers can reconcile the Zustand canvas store with the row.
>     24	 *
>     25	 * Used by Phase 4.2c async video flow: higgsfield-webhook flips status
>     26	 * `queued → success/failed/timeout`, this hook (a) refetches query caches and
>     27	 * (b) delegates the node-level status sync to the caller via `onUpdate`.
>     28	 *
>     29	 * Pattern mirrors `useMcorchMemory.ts:91-112` (canonical Realtime subscription).
>     30	 * RLS-aligned: `user_id=eq.<auth.uid>` filter ensures cross-tenant isolation at
>     31	 * the channel level. The callback is the single bridge between Realtime → Zustand,
>     32	 * keeping the store as the source of truth for node visual state.
>     33	 */
>     34	export function useCanvasJobsRealtime(

>     37	): void {

>     40	
>     41	  useEffect(() => {
>     42	    if (!user) return;
>     43	
>     44	    const channel = supabase
>     45	      .channel(`canvas_jobs_${user.id}`)
>     46	      .on(
>     47	        'postgres_changes',
>     48	        {
>     49	          event: 'UPDATE',
>     50	          schema: 'public',
>     51	          table: 'vm_canvas_executions',
>     52	          filter: `user_id=eq.${user.id}`,
>     53	        },
>     54	        (payload) => {
>     55	          const row = payload.new as CanvasExecutionRow;
>     56	          qc.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['canvas-execution', row.id] });

> const nid = (productKey: string, suffix: string) => `n_${productKey}_${suffix}`;
> 
> /**
>  * Build the ReactFlow graph (nodes + edges) for the campaign so the project becomes
>  * a first-class, visible canvas: base → variation (→ video). The already-generated
>  * images are attached via data.output.{imageUrl,videoUrl}, so nodes render the real
>  * creatives without re-spending. Handle ids mirror the node components
>  * (GenerateImageNode: input_prompt/output_image · ImageToVideoNode: input_image/output_video).
>  */
> function buildCampaignGraph(
>   product: Product,
>   baseUrl: string,
>   varUrl: string,
>   videoUrl: string | null,
>   seed: number,
> ) {
>   const now = new Date().toISOString();
>   const controls = { creativity: 0.7, resemblance: 0.3, hdr: 0.5, fractality: 0.3, sharpness: 0.8 };
>   const baseId = nid(product.key, "base");
>   const varId = nid(product.key, "variation");
> 
>   const nodes: Record<string, unknown>[] = [
>     {
>       id: baseId,
>       type: "generateImage",
>       position: { x: 40, y: 140 },
>       data: {
>         kind: "generateImage", label: "Base", modelId: "openrouter-gemini-image",
>         prompt: product.basePrompt, negativePrompt: "", aspectRatio: "16:9",
>         controls: { ...controls }, seed, fixedSeed: true, status: "success",
>         inheritStyle: false, output: { imageUrl: baseUrl, generatedAt: now },
>       },
>     },
>     {
>       id: varId,
>       type: "generateImage",
>       position: { x: 420, y: 140 },
>       data: {
>         kind: "generateImage", label: "Variação consistente", modelId: "openrouter-gemini-image",
>         prompt: product.variationPrompt, negativePrompt: "", aspectRatio: "16:9",
>         controls: { ...controls }, seed, fixedSeed: true, status: "success",
>         inheritStyle: false, output: { imageUrl: varUrl, generatedAt: now },
>       },
>     },
>   ];
> 
>   const edges: Record<string, unknown>[] = [
>     {
>       id: `e_${baseId}_${varId}`, type: "pipeline",
>       source: baseId, target: varId,
>       sourceHandle: "output_image", targetHandle: "input_prompt",
>     },
>   ];
> 
>   if (videoUrl) {
>     const vidId = nid(product.key, "video");
>     nodes.push({
>       id: vidId,
>       type: "imageToVideo",
>       position: { x: 800, y: 140 },
>       data: {
>         kind: "imageToVideo", label: "Vídeo (DoP Standard)", prompt: product.videoPrompt,
>         duration: 5, aspectRatio: "16:9", videoModel: "dop-standard-5s", motionStrength: 0.5,
>         status: "success", output: { videoUrl, generatedAt: now },
>       },
>     });
>     edges.push({
>       id: `e_${varId}_${vidId}`, type: "pipeline",
>       source: varId, target: vidId,
>       sourceHandle: "output_image", targetHandle: "input_image",
>     });
>   }
> 
>   return { nodes, edges };
> }
> 
> /** Persist nodes+edges into vm_canvas_projects.graph, preserving lanes/styleSettings.
>  *  Free-form layout (pipelineMode=false) so the short pipeline row renders cleanly. */
> async function persistGraph(admin: SupabaseClient, projectId: string, nodes: Record<string, unknown>[], edges: Record<string, unknown>[]): Promise<void> {
>   const { data: row } = await admin
>     .from("vm_canvas_projects").select("graph").eq("id", projectId).maybeSingle();
>   const current = (row?.graph && typeof row.graph === "object") ? row.graph : {};
>   const graph = { ...current, nodes, edges, pipelineMode: false };
>   const { error } = await admin.from("vm_canvas_projects").update({ graph }).eq("id", projectId);
>   if (error) throw new Error(`persistGraph failed: ${error.message}`);
> }
> 
> async function main() {
>   const productKey = process.argv[2];
>   const withVideo = process.argv.includes("--video");
>   const product = PRODUCTS[productKey];
>   if (!product) {
>     console.error(`Unknown product. Use one of: ${Object.keys(PRODUCTS).join(", ")}`);
>     process.exit(1);
>   }
> 
>   const admin = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, SERVICE_KEY, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
>   const userClient = createClient(SUPABASE_URL, PUBLISHABLE_KEY, { auth: { persistSession: false } });
> ```

> export function estimateNodeCost(data: CanvasNodeData): number {
>   switch (data.kind) {
>     case "generateImage":
>       return getImageModelInfo(data.modelId).coinCost;
>     case "sceneCompose":
>       // Never trust persisted data.cost — graphs saved before Amendment 2d-bis carry the old 18.
>       return SCENE_COMPOSE_COST;
>     case "styleTransfer":
>       return STYLE_TRANSFER_COST;
>     case "imageToVideo":
>       return getVideoModelInfo(data.videoModel).coinCost;
>     case "characterReference":
>       return 0;
>     case "productList":
>       return 0; // input node — cost is realized per item at the downstream generateImage (it.4a)
>     case "upscale":
>       return UPSCALE_COST[data.scale]; // it.4c — Replicate real-esrgan, priced by scale
>     case "voiceClone":
>       // qwen3-local = free self-host engine (Amendment 18, FR-SPACES-056 — 0 mco while the free
>       // directive stands); paid BYOK providers keep COIN_COSTS.VOICE_GENERATION on synthesize.
>       return data.provider === "qwen3-local" ? 0 : 36;
>     case "avatarIdentity":
>       return 0; // train (FR-AC-012) is gated OTD-AC-017; cost charged server-side when wired (OTD-AC-006)
>     case "publishWordpress":
>       return 0; // distribution sink — publishing is free (reuses per-user WP creds)
>     case "scriptGenerator":
>       return 2; // Roteiro — LLM script (mcoin-cost-calibration 4x-floor, OTD-CS-006)
>     case "cinematicVideo":
>       return 12; // COST.hyperframes — free FFmpeg render, prepaid mco (refund-on-failure)
>     case "splitScreen":
>       return 12; // COST.hyperframes — mesma fila/preço do cinematicVideo (Amendment 27 FR-SPACES-098)
>     case "carousel":
>       return 0; // Amendment 29 — rail de repurpose grátis (charged_mco=0); publicar/agendar é sink grátis
>     case "publishSocial":
>       return 0; // spaces-cadence FR-CAD-016 (Fatia 0 prereq): distribution SINK — was missing, made runAllCost NaN
>     case "cadence":
>       return 0; // spaces-cadence FR-CAD-001: arming a recurring plan is free (G7 is a per-CYCLE invariant, not the node)
>   }
> }

## 🤖 Claude *(17:14:49)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

I'm ready to write out the structured report with precise file references and line numbers. I need to map out the key architectural distinction between VIVO and MIRROR components, starting with the route definition in App.tsx and identifying which files are off-limits for modifications.

The CanvasEditorPage component is the main entry point, spanning lines 75-542 in the file. It orchestrates node and edge types, handles the hydration effect, manages store consumption, and sets up the layout with ReactFlow in the center flanked by left and right panels. There's also zoom compensation logic, auto-save functionality, node interaction handlers, and real-time job monitoring that all feed into the overall canvas experience.

For the Chat integration, I'm identifying three possible mount points: adding a ChatPanel as a third dock alongside the RightPanel in the desktop layout, embedding a floating Dialog-based window near the NodeConfigWindow, or adding another Sheet and FAB for mobile—though I need to be careful about positioning conflicts with the existing AddNodeFab.

On the store side, the chat MVP needs a few missing actions like removeNode, updateNodePosition, and bulk setNodes operations. The addRecipe method at line 106-107 is already set up as the exact seam an agent would use for bulk inserts. For persistence, there's a 3-second debounce on autosave with flush handlers on visibility changes and unmount, plus a getPersistedGraph function to retrieve saved state.

The Zundo temporal system only tracks nodes and edges through partialization with a 50-item limit and 500ms debounce, which means any chat-related fields I add to the store (like chatOpen or chatAnchorNodeId) automatically stay outside the undo history since they're not included in that partialization. They also won't persist unless explicitly added to getPersistedGraph, which they aren't. The autoSave effect dependencies need to include nodes, edges, viewport, pipelineMode, and language settings to capture all the state that should trigger a save.

Now I'm mapping out the registry and type system — there's a union of CanvasNodeKind types, CanvasNodeData variants, and node factories that generate IDs with an `n_` prefix plus 8 base36 characters, plus a searchable registry of node categories and entries. The NodeInspectorBody component dispatches based on the node type to render the appropriate inspector UI.

Looking at the inspector panels now — there's a NodeConfigWindow dialog with max width constraints, a right sidebar panel, and a NodeSpecsPanel with a customization button, along with shared inspector components like Section, CostPreview, InspectorHeader, and InheritanceBadge. The cost system tracks estimated costs per registry entry and displays them as badges on node cards, pulling from the estimateNodeCost function. Adding motion and styling details...

</details>

## 🤖 Claude *(17:15:25)*

<details>
<summary>💭 Thinking Process</summary>

Now I'm verifying the line numbers for the CSS references I'll be citing in the report, particularly checking where the glass-panel and glow-primary styles are defined in the index.css file. I've confirmed the CSS variable locations—`--glow-primary` is at line 70, and the glass panel utilities start at line 111 with the glow variants following. The motion-related classes are scattered across the file with `.mc-node-processing` at 262, `.mc-node-failed` at 343, and the reduced-motion query at 365-372. I also found the `::selection` styling at line 107. Now I'm ready to write up the comprehensive report in Portuguese since that's what the user requested.

The persisted node structure in the database stores each canvas node with an ID generated from base36 encoding, a type that must match one of the defined node kinds, position coordinates, and a data object containing the node's kind as a discriminant, label, status, and kind-specific fields. ReactFlow fields like `measured` dimensions and `selected` state also persist to the database through the change application process. Edges follow a similar pattern with an id combining source and target, optional type designation, and handle references for connecting input/output ports — the motion data gets injected separately outside the store so it doesn't persist. Node ids stay stable across saves for thread references.

Since node ids are already used as foreign keys in the execution and generation tables, a threads table keyed by project and node id would fit the existing pattern perfectly. The caveat is that node ids use random 8-character generation so they're only unique within a project scope, not globally.

The chat backend has an SSE endpoint that streams OpenAI-format deltas with messages, system prompt, and model selection, authenticated via JWT. It handles token metering and coin deduction after the stream completes, but currently lacks tool-calling support which would require adding the tools and tool_calls fields.

</details>

# 1. `src/pages/CanvasEditorPage.tsx` — layout e seams de montagem

- `:165-261` `CanvasEditorPage` — `useCanvasProject` + `useCanvasExecutions`, efeito de hidratação `:175-239`, `initialize(project.id, {...})` `:229-236`, `temporal.getState().clear()` após 600ms `:237`.
- `:120-147` `nodeWithSuccessOutput` / `nodeWithFailure` — reconciliação de execuções terminais.

**`CanvasShell` — consumo do store (`:263-579`):**
```
:272-274  nodes, edges, pipelineMode
:298-302  onNodesChange, onEdgesChange, onConnect, setViewport, selectNode
:323-324  openConfigModal, updateNodeData
:404      selectedNodeId
```

**Handlers de seleção (o gancho de "chat ancorado ao nó"):**
- `:347-353` `onNodeClick` → `selectNode(node.id)` + `setRightCollapsed(false)`.
- `:356-363` `onNodeDoubleClick` → `selectNode` + `openConfigModal()` + `setRightCollapsed(true)`.
- `:439` `onPaneClick={() => selectNode(null)}`.

**Layout (onde o chat monta):**
```
:411-425   <TopBar/>          (fora da row)
:427       <div className="flex min-h-0 flex-1">     ← A ROW
:428         {!isMobile && !leftCollapsed && <LeftPanel/>}
:430-532     <div ref={flowWrapperRef} className="relative flex-1 min-w-0">  ← wrapper do canvas
:431-470       <ReactFlow …/>
:472-474       <AddNodeFab iconOnly={isMobile}/> + <AddNodeCommand/>
:478-503       botões desktop de colapso (absolute left-2/right-2 top-2 z-10)
:506-531       FABs mobile (absolute bottom-4 left-4 · bottom-[4.75rem] right-4)
:535-541     {!isMobile && !rightCollapsed && <RightPanel/>}   ← DOCK DIREITO
:542       </div>
:544-563   {isMobile && <Sheet left/> + <Sheet right/>}
:565-569   <NodeConfigWindow/>    ← JANELA (Dialog)
:571-576   <StatusBar/>
```

**Três lugares plausíveis para o painel de chat:**
1. **Terceiro dock** — irmão do `RightPanel` em `:541`, dentro da mesma flex row. `<ChatDock/>` com `w-96 shrink-0 border-l`. Precisa de um `chatCollapsed` state ao lado de `:344-345` e um botão flutuante junto de `:478-503`.
2. **Janela flutuante** — irmão de `<NodeConfigWindow/>` em `:569`, copiando o padrão `Dialog` de `NodeConfigWindow.tsx:24-42`. Menor atrito: já existe o par `configModalOpen`/`openConfigModal`/`closeConfigModal` no store para clonar.
3. **Overlay dentro do wrapper** — dentro de `:430-532` como `absolute right-4 bottom-4` (glass, redimensionável). **Cuidado com colisão de FAB:** `bottom-4 right-4` já é o `AddNodeFab` (`AddNodeMenu.tsx:55`) e `bottom-[4.75rem] right-4` é o FAB de propriedades mobile (`:524`).

Mobile: adicionar um terceiro `<Sheet>` em `:544-563` + FAB em `:506-531` (usar `bottom-[9.5rem] right-4`, único slot livre à direita).

**Motion derivado (não tocar no store):** `:100-113` `nodeMotionClass` / `edgeMotionFor`; `:278-297` `motionNodes` / `motionEdges` injetam `className` e `data.motion` **fora** do store — comentário explícito em `:276-277`: *"getPersistedGraph must never serialize visual state"*. Mesma regra vale para qualquer estado de chat visual.

**Zoom-aware CSS var:** `:309-322` — `applyZoomCompensation` escreve `--rf-zoom-inv = clamp(1/zoom, 1, 12)` no wrapper (`:316`), semeada do viewport persistido em `:320-322`. Chamada em `onMove` `:440`.

**Realtime → store:** `:374-399` `useCanvasJobsRealtime(projectId, row => updateNodeData(...))`.

---

# 2. `src/store/useCanvasStore.ts` — shape do estado

**Interface `CanvasState` `:23-61`:**
```
:24  projectId: string | null
:25  nodes: CanvasNode[]
:26  edges: CanvasEdge[]
:27  viewport: Viewport
:28  selectedNodeId: string | null
:31  configModalOpen: boolean      ← PRECEDENTE EXATO para chatOpen/chatAnchorNodeId
:34-36 pipelineMode, lanes, styleSettings
```
Comentário `:29-30`: *"Transient UI state — outside temporal partialize (nodes/edges only) and not persisted."*

**Actions relevantes para o agente criar/editar nós:**
| Action | Linha | Uso pelo chat |
|---|---|---|
| `addNode(n)` | `:105` — `[...s.nodes, n]` + auto-seleciona | criar 1 nó |
| `addRecipe(nodes, edges)` | `:106-107` | **bulk insert — o seam ideal para "o agente montou um subgrafo"** |
| `updateNodeData(id, patch)` | `:109-114` — merge raso em `n.data` | editar campos de um nó |
| `selectNode(id)` | `:101` — fecha o config modal ao deselecionar | focar o nó da thread |
| `openConfigModal` / `closeConfigModal` | `:102-103` | padrão a clonar p/ chat |
| `removeSelected()` | `:116-126` — remove nó + edges incidentes | deletar |
| `onConnect(c)` | `:92-98` — usa `addEdge` do ReactFlow | conectar |
| `getPersistedGraph()` | `:143-149` | blob salvo |

**Lacunas para um agente:** não há `removeNode(id)` (só `removeSelected`), nem `addEdgeProgrammatic`, nem `setNodes` bulk. Escape hatch já usado em produção: `useCanvasStore.setState({ nodes })` em `CanvasEditorPage.tsx:210`.

**Zundo temporal `:151-164`:**
```
:153  partialize: (state) => ({ nodes: state.nodes, edges: state.edges })
:154  limit: 50
:156-162  handleSet: debounce 500ms (1 snapshot por rajada de drag)
:163  equality: a.nodes === b.nodes && a.edges === b.edges
```
Consequência prática: **qualquer campo novo no store (ex.: `chatMessages`, `chatOpen`) fica automaticamente fora do undo** — o partialize é allowlist. Um `undo` após o agente criar nós reverte a criação (desejável).

**Persistência em `vm_canvas_projects.graph` — é HOOK + DEBOUNCE, não middleware:**
- `src/hooks/useAutoSave.ts:8` `AUTOSAVE_DEBOUNCE_MS = 3_000`
- `:54-65` efeito que dispara o debounce; deps = `[projectId, nodes, edges, viewport, pipelineMode, lanes, styleSettings]` (`:65`) — estado de chat no store **não** dispara save.
- `:31-47` `flush` → `saveGraph.mutateAsync({ projectId, graph: getPersistedGraph(), viewport })`
- `:68-81` flush em `visibilitychange`/`pagehide`/unmount
- `:50-52` `saveNow()` (ligado ao botão em `CanvasEditorPage.tsx:419`)
- Mutation: `src/hooks/useCanvasStudio.ts:152-204`. **Guarda anti-clobber** `:166-182`: se `graph.nodes.length === 0`, lê antes de escrever e bloqueia a sobrescrita. `:191` faz o cast `graph as unknown as Json` num `TablesUpdate<"vm_canvas_projects">`.

---

# 3. Registry + tipos — os 6 seams

**SOP:** `docs/processes/engineer-spaces-node-authoring.md:14-23`
```
0  VIVO vs MIRROR        grep "spaces/:id" src/App.tsx
1  Tipo + factory        src/types/canvas.ts
2  Catálogo              src/lib/canvas-node-registry.ts (category ∈ NodeCategory)
3  Inspector + dispatcher + card
4  Despacho (node_run_id / edge-fn / fila)
5  Custo server-side (CREDIT_COSTS ou charged_mco:0)
6  Assets assinados owner-scoped (asset-url.ts)
```

**Seam 1 — `src/types/canvas.ts`:**
- `:26-45` `CanvasNodeKind` (19 kinds)
- `:47-55` `NodeStatus = idle|queued|running|success|failed|cancelled`

**Seam 2 — `src/lib/canvas-node-registry.ts`:**

- `:28-37` `NodeRegistryEntry { id, label, description, category, icon, estimatedCost, aliases, factory }`

# 4. Inspectors — onde o chat pode montar

**`RightPanel/NodeInspectorBody.tsx`** — dispatcher `kind → inspector`, cadeia ternária `:31-98`. `sceneNote` e `povVideo` caem no `: null` de `:98`. Duas assinaturas de props em uso:
- com créditos: `node={{id, data}} projectId creditsBalance onCreditsChanged` (generateImage, sceneCompose, styleTransfer, upscale, imageToVideo, voiceClone)
- sem: `node projectId` (o resto) · `videoRepurpose`/`transcribe` só recebem `node` (`:95,97`)

# 7. Chat UI existente — o que dá para reusar

**Sim, existem dois sistemas de chat.**

### (a) Core-Fable / AIOSTerminal — streaming real, linguagem MIV cyan
- **`src/components/core/AIOSTerminal.tsx`** (~2200 linhas), montado em `src/pages/CoreFablePage.tsx:200`
  - `:999-1044` **`MessageBubble`** — avatares `w-8 h-8 rounded-xl` cyan (`:1008-1010`, `:1039-1043`), bolha user `bg-cyan-900/20 border-cyan-500/20 rounded-tr-sm` vs assistant `bg-zinc-900/35 border-zinc-800/40 rounded-tl-sm` (`:1013-1019`), `max-w-[85%]`
  - `:961-996` **`FormattedMessageContent`** — markdown + code blocks + detecção de JSON (`:964-978`), cursor de streaming `█ animate-pulse text-cyan-400` (`:991-993`)
  - `:1837` render loop; `:1952-1975` input com botão stop/send; `:2002+` `ModelSelector` (glass: `bg-black/95 border-cyan-500/25 backdrop-blur-md`, `:2106`)
  - `:49` `ALL_MODELS` exportado e reusado em `AffiliateProductsPage`, `ModelArenaPage`, `SwarmModelSelector`
- **`src/hooks/useAiosTerminalChat.ts`** — `useAIOSChat`:
  - `:6-12` `AIOSMessage { id, role: "user"|"assistant"|"system", content, timestamp, isStreaming? }`
  - `:90-176` `sendMessage` (otimista: empurra user + assistant vazio em `:109`), abort em `:113`/`:178-180`
  - `:205-260` **`streamViaEdgeFunction`** — `fetch` para `${VITE_SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/aios-chat-stream`, body `{messages, system, model}`, `getReader()` + parse SSE `data:` → `choices[0].delta.content` (`:239-258`)
  - `:148-171` mapeamento de erro pt-BR (429/401/404)
  - `:48` injeta `useKnowledgeMesh` no system prompt (`<KNOWLEDGE_CONTEXT>`, `:130`) — **o precedente exato de "grounding do agente"**; para chat-por-nó, o análogo é injetar o grafo serializado.

### (b) ChatPage — canais/threads persistidos (sem LLM)
- **`src/pages/ChatPage.tsx`** (rota `src/App.tsx:177` `/dashboard/chat`) — 3 colunas: canais `:132-223`, threads `:226-292`, mensagens `:294-392`. Bolhas em `:356-362`, input em `:373-389` (Enter=send, Shift+Enter=nova linha, `:379-384`).
- **`src/hooks/useAiosWorkspaceChat.ts`** — tabelas `aios_channels` / `aios_threads` / `aios_messages`; shapes em `:5-32`; `Message` tem `sender_type: 'user'|'agent'`, `sender_id`, **`metadata: Record<string, unknown>`** (`:30`) e `postMessage` aceita `metadata` (`:130,142`). **Isso é o schema de threads mais próximo do que um chat-por-nó precisa** — bastaria `metadata.node_id`/`metadata.project_id`, ou uma tabela nova espelhando o padrão.

### (c) Input estético isolado
- **`src/components/ui/v0-ai-chat.tsx:74-200`** `VercelV0Chat` — textarea auto-resize (`:23-72` `useAutoResizeTextarea`), usado só no landing (`src/components/landing/HeroSection.tsx:118`). Reusável como input do painel.

### Backends LLM disponíveis
| Function | Contrato | Nota |
|---|---|---|
| `aios-chat-stream` | `:73-76` body `{messages, system, model}` · `:140-142` OpenRouter `stream:true` · `:214-216` `text/event-stream` · `:191-207` metering + `logAndDeduct` pós-stream | **Sem `tools`/tool_calls** — precisa ser estendido para o agente CRIAR nós via tool-calling |
| `aios-chat`, `aios-chat-gateway` | não-streaming | |
| `openrouter-swarm` | multi-modelo | |
| `agent-task-execute` | `:91-92` `{agent_id, task_prompt, capability_key, payload, idempotency_key}` · router de capabilities `:346+` · 402 em `:179,367` (`consumption_cap_reached`) · `:272-296` `calc_agent_cost_mcoin` + `deduct_mco_coins` | **Precedente de agente com custo/idempotência** |
| `generate-video-script`, `generate-script-package` | usados pelo `ScriptGeneratorInspector.tsx:66,93` | Único uso de LLM já dentro do canvas |
